\displaystyle\quad+M\chi_{\delta}\Bigl[\partial_{t}-\partial_{rr}-\Bigl(\frac{1}{r}+\frac{4r}{\lambda^{2}+r^{2}}\Bigr)\partial_{r}\Bigr]\tilde{\psi}_{2}+R(r,t),

where

\displaystyle R

\displaystyle=M\Bigl[\psi_{2}\partial_{t}\chi_{\delta}-2\partial_{r}\tilde{\psi}_{2}\partial_{r}\chi_{\delta}-\tilde{\psi}_{2}\Bigl(\partial_{rr}\chi_{\delta}+\frac{1}{r}\partial_{r}\chi_{\delta}+4\frac{r}{r^{2}+\lambda^{2}}\partial_{r}\chi_{\delta}\Bigr)\Bigr].

We have, by (
13.10
),

(13.13)

\displaystyle|R(r,t)|\leq C_{2}M\lambda^{2}\frac{1}{(t-t_{0}+A)^{a+1}(\log t)^{\beta}},

where
C_{2}
is independent of
M
(although it depends on
\delta
),
and is supported on
\delta\sqrt{t-t_{0}+A}\leq r\leq 2\delta\sqrt{t-t_{0}+A}
.

We claim that there is
M>0
and
\tilde{c}>0
so that for all
t_{0}
sufficiently large

(13.14)

\displaystyle\Bigl[\partial_{t}-\partial_{rr}-\Bigl(\frac{1}{r}+\frac{4r}{\lambda^{2}+r^{2}}\Bigr)\partial_{r}\Bigr]\tilde{\psi}

\displaystyle\geq\tilde{c}\frac{1}{t^{a}(\log t)^{\beta}(1+r/\sqrt{t})^{b}},

for all
r>0
,
t>t_{0}
.

Indeed, if
r\leq\delta\sqrt{t-t_{0}+A}
, then from (
13.12
), (
13.7
), (
13.11
) and (
13.9
) we get

\displaystyle\Bigl[\partial_{t}-\partial_{rr}-\Bigl(\frac{1}{r}+\frac{4r}{\lambda^{2}+r^{2}}\Bigr)\partial_{r}\bigr]\tilde{\psi}

\displaystyle\geq c\frac{1}{(t-t_{0}+A)^{a}(\log t)^{\beta}(1+\frac{r}{\sqrt{t-t_{0}+A}})^{b}}

\displaystyle\quad-C\frac{\lambda^{2}}{(r^{2}+\lambda^{2})}\frac{1}{(t-t_{0}+A)^{a-1}(\log t)^{\beta}}

\displaystyle\quad+M\chi_{\delta}\Bigl[\partial_{t}-\partial_{rr}-\Bigl(\frac{1}{r}+\frac{4r}{\lambda^{2}+r^{2}}\Bigr)\partial_{r}\Bigr]\tilde{\psi}_{2}

(13.15)

\displaystyle\geq c\frac{1}{(t-t_{0}+A)^{a}(\log t)^{\beta}(1+\frac{r}{\sqrt{t-t_{0}+A}})^{b}},

if
M\geq C
. Here we fix
M=C
.

If
\delta\sqrt{t-t_{0}+A}\leq r\leq 2\delta\sqrt{t-t_{0}+A}
, then by (
13.12
), (
13.7
), (
13.9
) and (
13.13
) we get

\displaystyle\Bigl[\partial_{t}-\partial_{rr}-\Bigl(\frac{1}{r}+\frac{4r}{\lambda^{2}+r^{2}}\Bigr)\partial_{r}\bigr]\tilde{\psi}

\displaystyle\geq c\frac{1}{(t-t_{0}+A)^{a}(\log t)^{\beta}(1+\frac{r}{\sqrt{t-t_{0}+A}})^{b}}-C_{2}M\lambda^{2}\frac{1}{(t-t_{0}+A)^{a+1}(\log t)^{\beta}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(t-t_{0}+A)^{a}(\log t)^{\beta}}\Bigl(\frac{c}{3^{b}}-\frac{C_{2}M\lambda^{2}}{t-t_{0}+A}\Bigr)

By taking
\frac{A}{\lambda(t_{0})^{2}}
large, we get

(13.16)

\displaystyle\Bigl[\partial_{t}-\partial_{rr}-\Bigl(\frac{1}{r}+\frac{4r}{\lambda^{2}+r^{2}}\Bigr)\partial_{r}\bigr]\tilde{\psi}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{c}{2}\frac{1}{(t-t_{0}+A)^{a}(\log t)^{\beta}(1+\frac{r}{\sqrt{t-t_{0}+A}})^{b}},

for
\delta\sqrt{t-t_{0}+A}\leq r\leq 2\delta\sqrt{t-t_{0}+A}
.

If
r\geq 2\delta\sqrt{t-t_{0}+A}
, by (
13.12
) and (
13.8
)

\displaystyle\Bigl[\partial_{t}-\partial_{rr}-\Bigl(\frac{1}{r}+\frac{4r}{\lambda^{2}+r^{2}}\Bigr)\partial_{r}\bigr]\psi_{1}

\displaystyle\geq c\frac{1}{(t-t_{0}+A)^{a}(\log t)^{\beta}(1+\frac{r}{\sqrt{t-t_{0}+A}})^{b}}

\displaystyle\quad-C\frac{\lambda^{2}}{r^{2}+\lambda^{2}}\frac{1}{(t-t_{0}+A)^{a}(\log t)^{\beta}}\frac{1}{(1+\frac{r^{2}}{t-t_{0}+A})^{b/2+1}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{(t-t_{0}+A)^{a}(\log t)^{\beta}(1+\frac{r}{\sqrt{t-t_{0}+A}})^{b}}\Bigl[c-C\frac{\lambda^{2}}{t-t_{0}+A}\Bigr]

(13.17)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{c}{2}\frac{1}{(t-t_{0}+A)^{a}(\log t)^{\beta}(1+\frac{r}{\sqrt{t-t_{0}+A}})^{b}}

if
\frac{A}{\lambda(t_{0})^{2}}
is sufficiently large.

Combining (
13.15
), (
13.16
) and (
13.17
) we deduce the estimate (
13.14
).

Let

\psi(x,t)=\tilde{\psi}(|x-\xi|,t).

Then by (
13.14
)

\displaystyle(\partial_{t}-L^{o})[\psi]

\displaystyle=\Bigl[\partial_{t}-\partial_{rr}-\Bigl(\frac{1}{r}+\frac{4r}{\lambda^{2}+r^{2}}\Bigr)\partial_{r}\Bigr]\tilde{\psi}-\partial_{r}\tilde{\psi}\frac{(x-\xi)\cdot\dot{\xi}}{|x-\xi|}

\displaystyle\geq\tilde{c}\frac{1}{(t-t_{0}+A)^{a}(\log t)^{\beta}(1+\frac{r}{\sqrt{t-t_{0}+A}})^{b}}-|\dot{\xi}|\,|\partial_{r}\tilde{\psi}|.

But

\displaystyle|\partial_{r}\psi|

\displaystyle\leq C\frac{1}{(t-t_{0}+A)^{a-1/2}(\log t)^{\beta}}\frac{1}{(1+\frac{r}{\sqrt{t-t_{0}+A}})^{b+1}}+C\frac{1}{(t-t_{0}+A)^{a-1}(\log t)^{\beta}}\frac{1}{\lambda}\frac{1}{(1+r/\lambda)^{3}}\chi_{\delta}(r,t)

\displaystyle\quad+C\frac{1}{\delta(t-t_{0}+A)^{a-1/2}(\log t)^{\beta}}\frac{1}{(1+r/\lambda)^{2}}\chi_{0}^{\prime}\Bigl(\frac{r}{\delta\sqrt{t-t_{0}+A}}\Bigr).

Using (
13.4
) we see that if
t_{0}
is sufficiently large,

\displaystyle(\partial_{t}-L^{o})[\psi]

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\tilde{c}}{2}\frac{1}{(t-t_{0}+A)^{a}(\log t)^{\beta}(1+\frac{r}{\sqrt{t-t_{0}+A}})^{b}}.

∎

A direct consequence of the proof of Proposition
13.1
(using the same barriers) is the following, for the initial value problem

(13.18)

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} \partial_{t}\phi^{o}&=L^{o}[\phi^{o}],\quad\text{in }\mathbb{R}^{2}\times(t_{0},\infty)\\
\phi^{o}(\cdot,t_{0})&=\phi^{o}_{0},\quad\text{in }\mathbb{R}^{2}.\end{aligned}\right.

Consider the norm

\displaystyle\|\phi^{o}_{0}\|_{*,b}=

\displaystyle\inf K\quad\text{such that }

\displaystyle|\phi^{o}_{0}(x)|\leq\frac{K}{(1+\frac{|x-\xi(0)|}{\sqrt{t-t_{0}+A}})^{b}}

where
b\in(2,6)
,
A>0
.

Proposition 13.2
.

Assume that
a,b
satisfy (
13.2
),
\frac{A}{\lambda(t_{0})^{2}}
is sufficiently large, and
\lambda,\xi
satisfy (
13.3
), (
13.4
).
Then there is a constant
C
so that for
t_{0}
sufficiently large and for
\|\phi^{o}_{0}\|_{*,b}<\infty
there exists a solution
\phi^{o}
of (
13.18
), which defines a linear operator of
\phi^{o}_{0}
and satisfies

\displaystyle\|\phi^{o}\|_{*,o}\leq CA^{a-1}(\log t_{0})^{\beta}\|\phi^{o}_{0}\|_{*,b}.

†

Data sharing not applicable.
